## 🔨 turn 361 (cycle 127) — 3D probe-vs-WCS anchor: REPRODUCED + ROOT-CAUSED, tried a fix, HUMAN RULED IT WRONG → REVERTED (clean, nothing shipped). The human re-framed the whole datum MODEL. NO CODE shipped (Option-A fix + its test reverted; git diff vs HEAD empty).

**THE DISPATCHED BUG:** the 3D preview shows the corner probe/tool NOT on the physical stock corner — offset by one stock width — when the stock DATUM is not the lower-left corner (human t360). REPRODUCED (drove the app, measured): the stock is datum-placed in the 3D (`pg.position = stock/2 − D`, D = the datum offset, gcodeViz3d.js:1150-1158) so its FL corner sits at partFrame (−100,0) for a front-right datum; BUT the probe markers/tool/path anchor to `this.starts` in the STOCK MIN-XY frame (frac anchor = fraction × stock from the FL corner, opSimStarts.js:172), so they render near partFrame (0,0) — one stock width off the datum-placed stock. "The model is correctly placed but the tool probes a phantom stock" (human).

**MY FIX (WRONG — reverted):** I made the probe stock present at MIN-XY (operator-relative in XY, like the Z top-at-0), so stock+tool coincide. Suite went green + the tool landed on the corner. BUT the human ruled it WRONG: it put part-zero at the min-XY corner regardless of the datum — "part zero datum in stock should MOVE the wcs." Reverted cleanly.

**THE HUMAN'S RE-FRAMED MODEL (the real spec — for the advisor):**
- The DATUM (stock modal) = part-zero = the WCS origin. Setting the datum MOVES part-zero to that corner/point. The 3D must keep the datum-placed stock (part-zero at the datum).
- The corner-pick circles are PHYSICAL stock corners. A probed corner is generally OFFSET from the WCS/part-zero; "the probe is not necessarily on the WCS." So the tool should sit on the chosen physical corner = `corner_physical − datum` relative to part-zero (offset from the WCS by the stock-size distance) — NOT un-shift the stock.
- **"Changing the datum should change the OFFSET COORD of the feature, NOT move the feature."** The feature (pocket/corner) is PHYSICAL — it stays put; changing the datum only re-computes its offset NUMBER (position relative to the new part-zero).
- **"It's probably missing a degree of variable."** The model CONFLATES the feature's PHYSICAL position with its datum-relative OFFSET. It needs BOTH: store the PHYSICAL position (datum-independent), and DERIVE the offset (= physical − datum) for display/emit. → **This implicates M2 (t357): I stored feature.pos DATUM-RELATIVE (the "wrong fix we did before"). Storing the offset means a datum change keeps the number → the feature MOVES. Correct model: store physical, derive the offset.**

**CONSEQUENCE / SCOPE for the advisor:** the correct fix is NOT a preview tweak — it's a MODEL change: features (and the corner-probe target) carry a PHYSICAL position; the datum/part-zero is a separate reference; the OFFSET is derived. Then (a) changing the datum re-derives the offset coord without moving the feature; (b) the 3D renders the probe at the physical corner offset from part-zero; (c) M2's datum-relative-stored pos needs reworking to physical-stored + derived-offset. The t359 crosshair-follows-datum (committed 1c4ba8b) is consistent with "datum = part-zero" but should be reviewed against the full model. Nothing was shipped this turn — clean slate for the model rework.

**MORE (human t361, refining):** (a) the PROBE PATH (the traced toolpath) is ALSO wrong, not just the tool/marker — "thats not fixed at all"; the whole probe geometry (path + tool + markers) is anchored in the min-XY frame, so ALL of it is offset from the datum-placed stock, and the fix must move the path too. (b) "or you're just missing it in the code" — the missing degree may be an EXISTING variable/handling in the code I'm not applying (e.g. the datum offset D is computed in setStock:1150 but never applied to the starts/path/tool), NOT necessarily a new data-model field. So: check whether D (or a datum→work-frame transform) already exists and simply isn't wired into the probe path/tool/markers.

**PASS BACK per human t361 ("send it back to advisor, tell him its wrong still"): the corner-datum/WCS handling is STILL WRONG. Root cause pinned: the datum-placed stock (pg.position uses D) is not matched by the probe PATH + tool + markers (anchored in the stock MIN-XY frame via this.starts, D never applied) → all offset by the datum. The datum offset D EXISTS (gcodeViz3d.js:1150) but is only applied to the stock, not the probe geometry. Reproduced the DISCONNECT precisely: an explicit workpiece feature `size:{40,30}` (cavity X 30..70) → the probe stopped at x=78/22 (the LEGACY 25% inset, cavity 20..80), NOT the feature. The 3D render already uses the feature (human confirmed); the probe-stop + 2D did not.

**CAUSE PINNED:** `engine/probeGeometry.js:104-105` (`stockProbeStop`) hard-coded the pocket cavity as `w = max(8, min(x,y)·0.25)` reading only `stock.shape` + the OUTER dims — its stale comment "same inset the 3D view renders" (the 3D was migrated to projectWorkpiece in the pivot, the probe-stop was not). And `layoutSpecFromOp` never drew the workpiece features at all.

**FIX (READ getWorkpiece().features — the stock-modal size):**
- **probeGeometry.js `stockProbeStop`**: the pocket branch now iterates `projectWorkpiece(stock).features` (inside cavities: box centred at f.pos, extent f.size, tool-centre stops tip-r short) instead of the 25% inset. A LEGACY pocket (no features[]) derives the SAME 25% inset via deriveLegacyFeatures → BYTE-IDENTICAL; a modal-defined size stops at THAT wall. (VERIFY: legacy → [78,22]; 40×30 → [68,32]; 20×20 → [58,42] — the probe respects the rendered size.)
- **panelTypes.js `layoutSpecFromOp`**: draws the workpiece INSIDE cavities via `workpieceBackdrop(getWorkpiece())` → the 2D layout SHOWS the pocket at the feature size (VERIFY: a 40×30 feature draws a `fc-feature-pocket` rect w:40 h:30 at the cavity pos — not the legacy 60×40; 1 live rect). Generic (only inside cavities draw → an outer boss/solid has none → unchanged for corner/edge). ONE source with the 3D + the probe-stop.

**VERIFY (real-symptom + assert-the-value):** opened the in-place Middle on a 40×30 pocket workpiece → the 2D DRAWS the 40×30 pocket + the probe STOPS at the 40×30 wall (both = the stock-modal size, not the hardcoded inset). Screenshot scratchpad/middle_featread_pocket.png (opened; HUMAN confirmed "ok good"). Emit BYTE-IDENTICAL (probe-stop + layout are PREVIEW/SIM-only; no reach derivation — #1 stays the max-probe param per the dispatch's defer). Regression: middle-center-sim + middle-circular-sim (pocket sims, legacy byte-identical) green. **FULL SUITE: 667 passed, 2 skipped, 0 failed** (probeGeometry + panelTypes are shared — no corner/edge regression: corner/edge use boss/solid stocks with no inside cavity).

**FOLLOW-UP (advisor AMENDMENT t384, incorporated) — the OFFSET must be followed too:** the amendment (from the human's live-eyeball) stressed the pocket must match the modal at its REAL pos+OFFSET (e.g. "20×29 at offset -34,-2"), not just the size. REPRODUCED: an OFF-CENTRE pocket (20×29 at pos {30,45} on a 100×80 stock — its cavity 20..40 in X does NOT contain the stock centre 50) → the 2D DRAW + the probe-stop CAVITY followed the offset (both read f.pos), BUT the sim START was the STOCK centre {50,40} = OUTSIDE the cavity → the probe-stop (which only fires from INSIDE the hole) never triggered → the probe ran to the OUTER edge [102,-2]. CAUSE: the sim-start CENTRE (`BUILT_IN.middle` + `makeProvider`'s `ctx.cx/cy`) was hardcoded to the STOCK centre. FIX (opSimStarts.js): a `insideCentre(stock)` helper (the inside feature's physical pos, via projectWorkpiece; null for a boss/no-inside → stock centre). `BUILT_IN.middle` cx/cy + `makeProvider`'s NEW `pcx/pcy` (used by the 'centre' anchor ONLY — 'edge'/'radial' keep the STOCK centre, so edge/corner are UNCHANGED) now = the pocket centre. VERIFY: the off-centre pocket → the sim start FOLLOWS to {30,45} (built-in AND twin match), the probe STOPS at [38,22] (the off-centre wall). Screenshot scratchpad/middle_offset_pocket.png (opened) — the 2D pocket + Start sit OFF-CENTRE, matching the modal. E2 (middle-sim-starts) + middle-center-sim + middle-circular-sim green (plain/legacy stocks: insideCentre null / f.pos = stock centre → UNCHANGED). **FULL SUITE: 667 pass** (opSimStarts is shared — scoped to the 'centre' anchor, so no edge/corner regression). Emit byte-identical (sim-start is preview-only). So the middle pocket (draw + start + probe-stop) now FOLLOWS the modal at pos + offset + size, pixel-for-pixel.

**DEFERRED (flagged): (c) RETIRE syncStockShape.** The in-place Middle (userOpView) NEVER called syncStockShape (it's built-in-middleView-only), so the human's in-place path already "doesn't mutate stock.shape" — the feature-read is complete for the in-place. Retiring syncStockShape from the built-in middleView (legacy shim) would BREAK the built-in pocket tests (middle-center-sim relies on it FORCING stock.shape='pocket' to make the stock a pocket) → needs those tests updated to set a pocket stock explicitly. Since the human is satisfied + it's a legacy-only cleanup with test churn, DEFERRED. The NO-POCKET case (a workpiece with no inside feature → the middle references the outer / an Edit-workpiece link): also deferred — today the user defines the pocket in the stock modal (which the feature-read now reads). **NEXT: retire syncStockShape (with built-in test updates) if wanted + the modal-3D-refresh fix (issue 3) + release (held).**

## 🔨 turn 387 (cycle 127) — MODAL 3D REFRESH BUG (issue 3, shipped V10.84, human t382) — the LAST flagged bug. FIXED: the stock modal's 3D pane now repaints LIVE on every edit.

**REPRODUCE + PIN (drove the app, per dispatch):** the stock modal's 3D depth pane updated its mesh on edit but LAGGED until a viewcube/orbit nudge. CONFIRMED the mechanism: the modal viz runs with `_animOn=false` (stockEditor.js:27 — no render LOOP); `GcodeViz3D.setStock` does NOT call `this.render()` (only rebuilds the mesh); `render()` does NOT guard on `_animOn` (a direct call draws a frame). render3d (stockEditor.js:213) called `viz.setStock` + re-fit ONLY on a datum/shape change (`fitAll`, which renders). So a plain DIMENSION edit (X/Y/Z) or a pocket drag → no key change → no fitAll → NO frame → the pane lagged; a viewcube click's render() refreshed it.

**FIX (stockEditor.js render3d):** after `setStock`, when render3d does NOT re-fit (`else` of the datum/shape-change branch), force `viz.render()` → the modal 3D repaints on EVERY edit. fitAll (datum/shape change) already renders; this covers the no-refit dimension/pocket edits. A harmless extra frame otherwise (we only draw on an edit — the loop stays off, no perf/context-leak: ONE reused GcodeViz3D).

**VERIFY (real-symptom, drove the app + PROVED the cause):** stock-modal-3d-refresh.spec — spy on `GcodeViz3D.render` scoped to the MODAL viz (container inside #se_3d). A DIMENSION (Z) edit → the modal render fires (dim>0 — the LIVE repaint); a shape change → repaints via fitAll (control). PROVED the fix is the cause: TEMP-disabling the `else`-render → the test FAILS (dim=0); restored → passes. Screenshot scratchpad/stock_modal_3d_refresh.png. **FULL SUITE: 668 passed, 2 skipped, 0 failed** (stockEditor is app-loaded; the change is one guarded render call — no regression). **This was the LAST flagged bug → the middle port + modal fixes are ready for the advisor's RELEASE.**

## 🔨 turn 389 (cycle 127) — LAST middle-port POLISH: Diag Travel (#21) / Diag Primary (#22) render READONLY (drag-driven by the ②, not competing editable inputs). Human t386: "the forms arent used, we can use the variable". Advisor lean = readonly (done).

**WHY:** the in-place Middle's diagTravel/diagPrimary bindings rendered as EDITABLE number fields, but the ② canvas drag (t383) is the SOLE source (tieDiagTravel/diagAim writes them) — a competing editable field is redundant/confusing (the OLD middleView HID the Diag Travel field). Advisor lean: READONLY display (shows the ②-derived value, not editable).

**FIX (a DECLARED `readonly` binding flag — general, reusable):**
- **ui/formWidgets.js** `numberWidget`: a binding with `readonly:true` → `inp.readOnly = true` + a title hint + a dimmed style. A PROGRAMMATIC write (the ② drag's `_writeParam` sets `.value` + dispatches 'input') STILL lands + reads back — readOnly blocks USER typing only.
- **blocks/dataOps/deriveBindings.js**: carry `readonly` + `readonlyHint` from the spec through to the binding (like label/help/when).
- **blocks/dataOps/middleData.js**: `diagTravel` + `diagPrimary` specs → `readonly: true` + a readonlyHint ("Set by dragging the ② handle on the 2D canvas").

**VERIFY (real-symptom, drove the app):** middle-diag-aim.spec (new test) — open the in-place Middle (boss + probe-both + trans-auto) → the Diag Travel + Diag Primary fields render + are `readOnly=true`; a programmatic write ('42', mimicking the ② drag) STILL lands on the readonly field. Emit BYTE-IDENTICAL (form-display-only — no emit/binding-value change; middle-data-emit both tests green). corner/edge unregressed (the `readonly` flag only applies to bindings that declare it → their number fields are UNCHANGED). **FULL SUITE: 668 passed, 2 skipped, 1 flaky (middle-animator stroke-dashoffset timing — passed on retry), 0 real failures.** **This was the LAST middle-port polish → the middle port + modal fixes are ready for the advisor's RELEASE.**

## 🔨 turn 391 (cycle 127) — KNOB-BINDING PROJECTION (human: "do knob, it helps me troubleshoot to use Blocks") — GATE FINDING = SMALL. A data-op's form-bound params now open in the Blocks tab PRE-TICKED as knobs (a binding IS a knob — same record).

**GATE FINDING (dispatch asked which case, small vs large):** SMALL. A data-op in Blocks is NOT an opaque leaf — `op:user_middle_data` expands to `user_root` → the full atom stack (~30 assign atoms + probe/move/flow), each value-bearing assign already carrying a devMode EXPOSE_/PNAME_/WIDGET_ knob row. So "project the form bindings onto the atoms" = SEED each bound atom's `_expose` (the same record devMode reads to pre-tick), which round-trips through the EXISTING stackBridge `_expose` channel. No new block type, no new round-trip channel, no schema change — a projection over machinery that already exists. Advisor decides bundle-into-release vs separate; the change is self-contained (opSession + one test).

**MECHANISM (a projection, not a new declaration — reuses the EXISTING _expose round-trip):**
- **blocks/opSession.js `buildActiveOpStack`**: before `makeOp`, call `seedKnobExpose(op.type, bare)`. It finds the user-op def (listUserOps), takes its value bindings (bindingSpecs by macro-var identity, else legacy `bindings` by blockIndex), locates each bound atom in the flattened stack, and sets `rec._expose[FN(key)] = {p:<param>, w:'number'}` — ONLY on atoms whose bound field is a literal number (skips expression atoms like `#7=[0-#1]`). `_expose` rides `block.data` (stackBridge.js:238) → devMode augment/restoreExpose reads `blk.data._expose[vk]` → ticks EXPOSE_<vk>=TRUE + names PNAME_<vk>. Display/provenance ONLY — never touches the emit.

**VERIFY (real-symptom — drove the Blocks tab via window.__blkws + assert-the-value):** blocks-knob-binding.spec (new) — insert a data-op (user_corner_data) → open the Blocks tab → read the RENDERED assign blocks' fields: the bound #1 (dist) atom has EXPOSE_VALUE='TRUE' (PRE-TICKED) + PNAME_VALUE='dist'; the bound scalars (dist/f_fast/f_slow/port/radius/retract) all pre-tick with THEIR param names (the form-exposed set); an EXPRESSION atom (#7=[0-#1]) is NOT ticked. Model-level (earlier this turn): middle's 8 bound scalars (#1-6/#17/#18) all seed `_expose`, #7 (expression) skipped, `emitByteIdentical:true`. GENERAL — any data-op (corner verified in-render, middle at the model); no per-op code. **FULL SUITE: 670 passed, 2 skipped, 0 failed** (opSession is shared by every op-build path; the seed is a no-op for non-user ops + guarded on numeric bound fields → no corner/edge/emit regression). Emit byte-identical (the knob is metadata on block.data, invisible to emitMapped).

**NET:** open any ported data-op (corner/edge/middle) in Blocks → its form knobs are already ticked + named, so the human can troubleshoot the atom stack with the form's params surfaced. Middle port + modal fixes + this = ready for RELEASE. **PASS BACK: the GATE FINDING (SMALL) + the ticked knobs — the advisor decides bundle-into-release vs a separate task.**
